maximum time span for an event\n    min_stations = 3    # minimum number of stations required\n    max_time_diff_p_s = 30.0  # Maximum expected S-P time\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(all_picks):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_picks = [all_picks.iloc[i]]\n        event_start_time = all_picks.iloc[i]['timestamp']\n        event_stations = {all_picks.iloc[i]['station']}\n\n        # Look for additional picks within the time window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(all_picks):\n            pick = all_picks.iloc[j]\n            time_diff = (pick['timestamp'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add this pick to the event\n                event_picks.append(pick)\n                event_stations.add(pick['station'])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough stations\n        if len(event_stations) >= min_stations:\n            # Calculate a robust event time using weighted average\n            # Weight by probability\n            times = []\n            weights = []\n\n            for pick in event_picks:\n                # Convert to timestamp\n                ts = pick['timestamp'].timestamp()\n                times.append(ts)\n                weights.append(pick['prob'])\n\n            # Weighted average\n            if len(times) > 0:\n                avg_time = np.average(times, weights=weights)\n                event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(avg_time, tz=timezone.utc)\n\n                # Store event\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time.isoformat().replace('+00:00', ''),\n                    'num_stations': len(event_stations),\n                    'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n                    'p_picks': sum(1 for p in event_picks if p['phase'] == 'P'),\n                    's_picks': sum(1 for p in event_picks if p['phase'] == 'S')\n                })\n\n        # Move to the next unprocessed pick\n        i = j\n\n    print(f\"   Associated {len(events)} events\")\n\n    # Print event summary\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        print(\"\\n   Event summary:\")\n        for i, event in enumerate(events[:10]):  # Show first 10\n            print(f\"     Event {i+1}: {event['time']} | {event['num_stations']} stations | \"\n                  f\"{event['num_picks']} picks (P:{event['p_picks']}, S:{event['s_picks']})\")\n        if len(events) > 10:\n            print(f\"     ... and {len(events) - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add this pick to the event\n                event_picks.append(pick)\n                event_stations.add(pick['station'])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough stations\n        if len(event_stations) >= min_stations:\n            # Calculate a robust event time using weighted average\n            # Weight by probability\n            times = []\n            weights = []\n\n            for pick in event_picks:\n                # Convert to timestamp\n                ts = pick['timestamp'].timestamp()\n                times.append(ts)\n                weights.append(pick['prob'])\n\n            # Weighted average\n            if len(times) > 0:\n                avg_time = np.average(times, weights=weights)\n                event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(avg_time, tz=timezone.utc)\n\n                # Store event\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time.isoformat().replace('+00:00', ''),\n                    'num_stations': len(event_stations),\n                    'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n                    'p_picks': sum(1 for p in event_picks if p['phase'] == 'P'),\n                    's_picks': sum(1 for p in event_picks if p['phase'] == 'S')\n                })\n\n        # Move to the next unprocessed pick\n        i = j\n\n    print(f\"   Associated {len(events)} events\")\n\n    # Print event summary\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        print(\"\\n   Event summary:\")\n        for i, event in enumerate(events[:10]):  # Show first 10\n            print(f\"     Event {i+1}: {event['time']} | {event['num_stations']} stations | \"\n                  f\"{event['num_picks']} picks (P:{event['p_picks']}, S:{event['s_picks']})\")\n        if len(events) > 10:\n            print(f\"     ... and {len(events) - 10} more events\")\nelse:\n    print(\"   No picks to associate!\")\n    events = []\n\n# Output results\nprint(\"\\n8. Writing results...\")\nif len(events) > 0:\n    # Create results DataFrame with just the time column (as required)\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame([{'time': e['time']} for e in events])\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    print(f\"   Wrote {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\n    # Also save detailed results for analysis\n    detailed_results = pd.DataFrame(events)\n    detailed_results.to_csv('/root/detailed_results.csv', index=False)\n    print(f\"   Detailed results saved to /root/detailed_results.csv\")\nelse:\n    # Create empty results file\n    pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    print(\"   No events found - created empty results file\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"PIPELINE COMPLETE\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"\\nSummary:\")\nprint(f\"  - Total traces processed: {len(st)}\")\nprint(f\"  - Total picks found: {len(picks_df) if len(picks_df) > 0 else 0}\")\nprint(f\"  - Events detected: {len(events)}\")\nprint(f\"  - Results written to: /root/results.csv\")","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Loading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/stations.csv')\nprint(f\"   Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels from {stations_df['station'].nunique()} stations\")\n\n# Load waveform data\nprint(\"\\n2. Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read('/root/data/wave.mseed')\nprint(f\"   Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\nprint(f\"   Time range: {st[0].stats.starttime} to {st[0].stats.endtime}\")\nprint(f\"   Duration: {st[0].stats.endtime - st[0].stats.starttime:.1f} seconds\")\n\n# Group traces by station and select vertical channel for P-wave picking\nprint(\"\\n3. Preprocessing waveforms...\")\ntraces_for_picking = obspy.Stream()\nfor tr in st:\n    # Keep only vertical channels for P-wave picking (BHZ, HHZ, EHZ)\n    if tr.stats.channel in ['BHZ', 'HHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n        # Detrend and taper\n        tr.detrend(type='linear')\n        tr.detrend(type='demean')\n        tr.taper(type='hann', max_percentage=0.05)\n        traces_for_picking.append(tr)\n\nprint(f\"   Using {len(traces_for_picking)} vertical traces for P-wave picking\")\n\n# Load and prepare the phase picker model\nprint(\"\\n4. Find peaks above threshold\n        from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n        peaks, properties = find_peaks(np.abs(data), height=threshold, distance=50)\n\n        for peak in peaks:\n            peak_time = tr.stats.starttime + peak / tr.stats.sampling_rate\n            prob = min(properties['peak_heights'][list(peaks).index(peak)] / (10 * rms), 1.0)\n\n            picks.append({\n                'timestamp': peak_time,\n                'prob': prob,\n                'phase': 'P',  # Assume vertical channel = P\n                'station': tr.stats.station,\n                'network': tr.stats.network,\n                'channel': tr.stats.channel\n            })\n\n    print(f\"   Found {len(picks)} picks using amplitude method\")\n\n# Also try picking S-waves from horizontal channels\nprint(\"\\n6. Picking S-waves from horizontal channels...\")\ntry:\n    horizontal_traces = obspy.Stream()\n    for tr in st:\n        if tr.stats.channel in ['BHE', 'HHE', 'HNE', 'BHN', 'HHN', 'HNN']:\n            tr.detrend(type='linear')\n            tr.detrend(type='demean')\n            tr.taper(type='hann', max_percentage=0.05)\n            horizontal_traces.append(tr)\n\n    if len(horizontal_traces) > 0 and len(picks) > 0:\n        print(f\"   Using {len(horizontal_traces)} horizontal traces for S-wave picking\")\n\n        # For horizontal channels, focus on S-wave detection\n        s_picks = []\n\n        # Use the same model or a simpler picker for S-waves\n        for tr in horizontal_traces:\n            # Compute RMS and find peaks\n            data = tr.data\n            rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(data**2))\n            threshold = 2.5 * rms\n\n            from scipy.signal import find_peaks\n            peaks, properties = find_peaks(np.abs(data), height=threshold, distance=50)\n\n            for peak in peaks:\n                peak_time = tr.stats.starttime + peak / tr.stats.sampling_rate\n                prob = min(properties['peak_heights'][list(peaks).index(peak)] / (8 * rms), 1.0)\n\n                s_picks.append({\n                    'timestamp': peak_time,\n                    'prob': prob,\n                    'phase': 'S',\n                    'station': tr.stats.station,\n                    'network': tr.stats.network,\n                    'channel': tr.stats.channel\n                })\n\n        # Add S picks to the main picks list\n        picks.extend(s_picks)\n        print(f\"   Added {len(s_picks)} S-wave picks\")\n\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f\"   S-wave picking failed: {e}\")\n\n# Convert picks to DataFrame and filter\nif len(picks) > 0:\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(picks)\n\n    # Remove duplicate picks (same station, same time, same phase)\n    picks_df = picks_df.drop_duplicates(\n        subset=['station', 'timestamp', 'phase'],\n        keep='first'\n    )\n\n    # Sort by timestamp\n    picks_df = picks_df.sort_values('timestamp').reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    print(f\"\\n   Total unique picks: {len(picks_df)}\")\n    print(f\"   P picks: {sum(1 for p in picks if p['phase'] == 'P')}\")\n    print(f\"   S picks: {sum(1 for p in picks if p['phase'] == 'S')}\")\n\n    # Show sample picks\n    print(\"\\n   Sample picks:\")\n    for i in range(min(5, len(picks_df))):\n        pick = picks_df.iloc[i]\n        print(f\"     {pick['timestamp']} | {pick['phase']} | {pick['station']} | prob={pick['prob']:.3f}\")\nelse:\n    print(\"   No picks found!\")\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame()\n\n# Phase Association\nprint(\"\\n7. Associating phases to events...\")\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    events = []\n\n    # Create station metadata\n    station_info = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        station_id = f\"{row['network']}.{row['station']}\"\n        station_info[station_id] = {\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'elevation': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n    # Simple but effective association algorithm\n    # Group picks by time and require multiple stations\n\n    # Sort picks by timestamp\n    all_picks = picks_df.sort_values('timestamp').reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # Parameters for association\n    time_window = 15.0  # seconds - maximum time span for an event\n    min_stations = 3    # minimum number of stations required\n    max_time_diff_p_s = 30.0  # Maximum expected S-P time\n\n    i = 0\n    while i < len(all_picks):\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_picks = [all_picks.iloc[i]]\n        event_start_time = all_picks.iloc[i]['timestamp']\n        event_stations = {all_picks.iloc[i]['station']}\n\n        # Look for additional picks within the time window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < len(all_picks):\n            pick = all_picks.iloc[j]\n            time_diff = (pick['timestamp'] - event_start_time).total_seconds()\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                # Add this pick to the event\n                event_picks.append(pick)\n                event_stations.add(pick['station'])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough stations\n        if len(event_stations) >= min_stations:\n            # Calculate a robust event time using weighted average\n            # Weight by probability\n            times = []\n            weights = []\n\n            for pick in event_picks:\n                # Convert to timestamp\n                ts = pick['timestamp'].timestamp()\n                times.append(ts)\n                weights.append(pick['prob'])\n\n            # Weighted average\n            if len(times) > 0:\n                avg_time = np.average(times, weights=weights)\n                event_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(avg_time, tz=timezone.utc)\n\n                # Store event\n                events.append({\n                    'time': event_time.isoformat().replace('+00:00', ''),\n                    'num_stations': len(event_stations),\n                    'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n                    'p_picks': sum(1 for p in event_picks if p['phase'] == 'P'),\n                    's_picks': sum(1 for p in event_picks if p['phase'] == 'S')\n                })\n\n        # Move to the next unprocessed pick\n        i = j\n\n    print(f\"   Associated {len(events)} events\")\n\n    # Print event summary\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        print(\"\\n   Event summary:\")\n        for i, event in enumerate(events[:10]):  # Show first 10\n            print(f\"     Event {i+1}: {event['time']} | {event['num_stations']} stations | \"\n                  f\"{event['num_picks']} picks (P:{event['p_picks']}, S:{event['s_picks']})\")\n        if len(events) > 10:\n            print(f\"     ... and {len(events) - 10} more events\")\nelse:\n    print(\"   No picks to associate!\")\n    events = []\n\n# Output results\nprint(\"\\n8. Writing results...\")\nif len(events) > 0:\n    # Create results DataFrame with just the time column (as required)\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame([{'time': e['time']} for e in events])\n    results_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    print(f\"   Wrote {len(events)} events to /root/results.csv\")\n\n    # Also save detailed results for analysis\n    detailed_results = pd.DataFrame(events)\n    detailed_results.to_csv('/root/detailed_results.csv', index=False)\n    print(f\"   Detailed results saved to /root/detailed_results.csv\")\nelse:\n    # Create empty results file\n    pd.DataFrame({'time': []}).to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\n    print(\"   No events found - created empty results file\")\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"PIPELINE COMPLETE\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nprint(f\"\\nSummary:\")\nprint(f\"  - Total traces processed: {len(st)}\")\nprint(f\"  - Total picks found: {len(picks_df) if len(picks_df) > 0 else 0}\")\nprint(f\"  - Events detected: {len(events)}\")\nprint(f\"  - Results written to: /root/results.csv\")"}}

event_start_time\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                event_picks.append(pick)\n                event_stations.add(pick['station'])\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        if len(event_stations) >= min_stations:\n            times = all_picks.iloc[i:j]['timestamp'].values\n            weights = all_picks.iloc[i:j]['prob'].values\n            avg_time = np.average(times, weights=weights)\n\n            events.append({\n                'time': datetime.fromtimestamp(avg_time, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat().replace('+00:00', ''),\n                'timestamp': avg_time,\n                'num_stations': len(event_stations),\n                'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n                'p_picks': sum(1 for p in event_picks if p['phase'] == 'P'),\n                's_picks': sum(1 for p in event_picks if p['phase'] == 'S')\n            })\n\n        i = j\n\n    print(f\"   Initial events: {len(events)}\")\n\n    # Merge nearby events\n    if len(events) > 1:\n        events_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n        events_df = events_df.sort_values('timestamp').reset_index(drop=True)\n\n        merged = []\n        i = 0\n        while i < len(events_df):\n            cluster = [events_df.iloc[i]]\n            cluster_time = events_df.iloc[i]['timestamp']\n            total_p_picks = events_df.iloc[i]['p_picks']\n            total_s_picks = events_df.iloc[i]['s_picks']\n\n            j = i + 1\n            while j < len(events_df):\n                time_diff = events_df.iloc[j]['timestamp'] - cluster_time\n                if time_diff <= 5.0:\n                    cluster.append(events_df.iloc[j])\n                    total_p_picks += events_df.iloc[j]['p_picks']\n                    total_s_picks += events_df.iloc[j]['s_picks']\n                    cluster_time = events_df.iloc[j]['timestamp']\n                    j += 1\n                else:\n                    break\n\n            all_times = [e['timestamp'] for e in cluster]\n            merged_time = np.mean(all_times)\n\n            merged.append({\n                'time': datetime.fromtimestamp(merged_time, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat().replace('+00:00', ''),\n                'timestamp': merged_time,\n                'num_stations': sum(e['num_stations'] for e in cluster),\n                'num_picks': sum(e['num_picks'] for e in cluster),\n                'p_picks': total_p_picks,\n                's_picks': total_s_picks\n            })\n\n            i = j\n\n        events = merged\n        print(f\"   After clustering: {len(events)} events\")\n\n    print(f\"   Final events: {len(events)}\")\n\n    if len(events) > 0:\n        print(\"\\n   Event summary:\")\n        for i, event in enumerate(events[:15]):\n            print(f\"     Event {i+1}: {event['time']} | {event['num_stations']} stations | \"\n                  f\"{event['num_picks']} picks (P:{event['p_picks']}, S:{event['s_picks']})\")\n        if len(events) > 15:\n            print(f\"     ... and {len(events) - Preprocessing waveforms...\")\nvertical_traces = obspy.Stream()\nhorizontal_traces = obspy.Stream()\n\nfor tr in st:\n    if tr.stats.channel in ['BHZ', 'HHZ', 'HNZ', 'EHZ']:\n        tr_copy = tr.copy()\n        tr_copy.detrend(type='linear')\n        tr_copy.detrend(type='demean')\n        tr_copy.taper(type='hann', max_percentage=0.05)\n        vertical_traces.append(tr_copy)\n    elif tr.stats.channel in ['BHE', 'HHE', 'HNE', 'BHN', 'HHN', 'HNN']:\n        tr_copy = tr.copy()\n        tr_copy.detrend(type='linear')\n        tr_copy.detrend(type='demean')\n        tr_copy.taper(type='hann', max_percentage=0.05)\n        horizontal_traces.append(tr_copy)\n\nprint(f\"   Vertical traces: {len(vertical_traces)}\")\nprint(f\"   Horizontal traces: {len(horizontal_traces)}\")\n\n# Load EQTransformer model\nprint(\"\\n4. Loading EQTransformer model...\")\nmodel = EQTransformer.from_pretrained('original')\nprint(f\"   Model loaded (sampling rate: {model.sampling_rate} Hz)\")\nmodel.eval()\n\n# Phase picking\nprint(\"\\n5. Picking P and S phases using EQTransformer...\")\n\nall_picks = []  # Will hold tuples of (timestamp, prob, phase, station, network, channel)\n\n# Function to extract peaks from probability traces\ndef extract_picks(prob_trace, phase_type, threshold=0.3):\n    \"\"\"Extract peak picks from probability trace\"\"\"\n    picks_list = []\n    prob_data = prob_trace.data\n\n    if len(prob_data) == 0:\n        return picks_list\n\n    # Smooth the probability trace\n    window = 50  # 0.5 seconds at 100 Hz\n    smoothed = np.convolve(prob_data, np.ones(window)/window, mode='same')\n\n    # Find peaks\n    peaks, properties = find_peaks(smoothed, height=threshold, distance=100, prominence=0.1)\n\n    for peak in peaks:\n        peak_time = prob_trace.stats.starttime.timestamp + peak / prob_trace.stats.sampling_rate\n        prob = float(smoothed[peak])\n\n        picks_list.append({\n            'timestamp': peak_time,\n            'prob': prob,\n            'phase': phase_type,\n            'station': prob_trace.stats.station,\n            'network': prob_trace.stats.network,\n            'channel': prob_trace.stats.channel\n        })\n    return picks_list\n\n# Process traces one by one to handle sanitization issues\nprint(\"   Processing vertical channels for P/S picks...\")\nfor tr in vertical_traces:\n    single_tr = obspy.Stream([tr])\n    ann = model.annotate(single_tr)\n\n    if len(ann) >= 3:\n        p_trace = ann[1]  # P probability\n        s_trace = ann[2]  # S probability\n\n        # Extract P picks\n        p_picks = extract_picks(p_trace, 'P', threshold=0.2)\n        all_picks.extend(p_picks)\n\n        # Extract S picks\n        s_picks = extract_picks(s_trace, 'S', threshold=0.15)\n        all_picks.extend(s_picks)\n\nprint(f\"   Picks from vertical channels: P={sum(1 for p in all_picks if p['phase'] == 'P')}, \"\n      f\"S={sum(1 for p in all_picks if p['phase'] == 'S')}\")\n\n# Process horizontal traces for additional S-wave picks\nprint(\"   Processing horizontal channels for S picks...\")\nfor tr in horizontal_traces:\n    single_tr = obspy.Stream([tr])\n    ann = model.annotate(single_tr)\n\n    if len(ann) >= 3:\n        s_trace = ann[2]  # S probability\n        s_picks = extract_picks(s_trace, 'S', threshold=0.15)\n        all_picks.extend(s_picks)\n\nprint(f\"   Total picks: P={sum(1 for p in all_picks if p['phase'] == 'P')}, \"\n      f\"S={sum(1 for p in all_picks if p['phase'] == 'S')}\")\n\n# Process picks\nif len(all_picks) > 0:\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\n    picks_df = picks_df.sort_values('timestamp').reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # Filter out very low probability picks\n    min_prob = 0.2\n    picks_df = picks_df[picks_df['prob'] >= min_prob].reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    n_p = sum(1 for p in picks_df['phase'] == 'P')\n    n_s = sum(1 for p in picks_df['phase'] == 'S')\n\n    print(f\"\\n   After filtering (prob >= {min_prob}): {len(picks_df)} picks\")\n    print(f\"   P picks: {n_p}\")\n    print(f\"   S picks: {n_s}\")\n\n    # Get time range\n    min_time = picks_df['timestamp'].min()\n    max_time = picks_df['timestamp'].max()\n    print(f\"   Time range: {min_time:.2f} to {max_time:.2f} ({max_time-min_time:.1f} seconds)\")\nelse:\n    print(\"   No picks found!\")\n    picks_df = pd.DataFrame()\n\n# Phase Association\nprint(\"\\n6. Associating phases to events...\")\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    events = []\n\n    # Sort picks by timestamp\n    all_picks_sorted = picks_df.sort_values('timestamp').reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # Parameters\n    time_window = 15.0  # seconds - wider window to capture full event\n    min_stations = 3    # minimum number of stations required\n\n    i = 0\n    n_picks = len(all_picks_sorted)\n\n    while i < n_picks:\n        # Start a new potential event\n        event_picks = [all_picks_sorted.iloc[i]]\n        event_start_time = all_picks_sorted.iloc[i]['timestamp']\n        event_stations = {all_picks_sorted.iloc[i]['station']}\n        p_count = 1 if all_picks_sorted.iloc[i]['phase'] == 'P' else 0\n        s_count = 1 if all_picks_sorted.iloc[i]['phase'] == 'S' else 0\n\n        # Look for additional picks within the time window\n        j = i + 1\n        while j < n_picks:\n            pick = all_picks_sorted.iloc[j]\n            time_diff = pick['timestamp'] - event_start_time\n\n            if time_diff <= time_window:\n                event_picks.append(pick)\n                event_stations.add(pick['station'])\n                if pick['phase'] == 'P':\n                    p_count += 1\n                else:\n                    s_count += 1\n                j += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        # Check if we have enough stations\n        if len(event_stations) >= min_stations:\n            # Calculate weighted average event time\n            times = all_picks_sorted.iloc[i:j]['timestamp'].values\n            weights = all_picks_sorted.iloc[i:j]['prob'].values\n            avg_time = np.average(times, weights=weights)\n\n            events.append({\n                'time': datetime.fromtimestamp(avg_time, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat().replace('+00:00', ''),\n                'timestamp': avg_time,\n                'num_stations': len(event_stations),\n                'num_picks': len(event_picks),\n                'p_picks': p_count,\n                's_picks': s_count\n            })\n\n        i = j\n\n    print(f\"   Initial events: {len(events)}\")\n\n    # Merge nearby events (within 10 seconds to be conservative)\n    if len(events) > 1:\n        events_df = pd.DataFrame(events)\n        events_df = events_df.sort_values('timestamp').reset_index(drop=True)\n\n        merged = []\n        i = 0\n        while i < len(events_df):\n            cluster = [events_df.iloc[i]]\n            cluster_time = events_df.iloc[i]['timestamp']\n            total_p_picks = events_df.iloc[i]['p_picks']\n            total_s_picks = events_df.iloc[i]['s_picks']\n            total_stations = events_df.iloc[i]['num_stations']\n\n            j = i + 1\n            while j < len(events_df):\n                time_diff = events_df.iloc[j]['timestamp'] -
